The blast's second diff (FIS) is the same class one level up: "(ii) sale of all of its equity interests ..." is item (ii) of a two-item transaction list, lowercase-led, and the old code dropped it — the fix rescues list items, not just bullet tails. The repair's 30 skips are not failures: the batch-174 gates (headline verbatim, stored-core preservation, residue scan, live==blast) correctly refuse section-pick divergences (BLK, DASH, DLTR, NFLX, KKR), truncated stored headlines (DRI, LLY, MRK), live residue (AXP, CNC, KMX, PAYX, UAL) and real content drops (AWK, C, CEG, CSX, F) — each is a content question for a later batch, not a repair to force through.

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, baseline `/tmp/blast176c/new`, new `/tmp/blast176c/new2`): 2 diffs, both intended rescues (ANET (MRC) tail, FIS (ii) list item), 1,697 byte-identical. 8 new tests; full suite 1286 green. The pin is behavioral: on stashed old code all three attested lines open footnote definitions and the Arista end-to-end drops the (MRC) tail.

**Related:** Closes the ANET/MRK/PLUG paren-continuation defect and the FIS "(ii) sale" gap. Still open: the 31-row / 34-line audit remainder (KKR ×4, BLK ×3, MRK ×2, AWK ×2, CSX ×2, CEG ×2, KMX ×2, plus singles) with per-row skip reasons in the batch-176 repair run; AFRM's page-header-prefix false positive (separate design: stripping running headers from flattened letter mega-lines); the 500-char cap's two survivors; MCO's table-header lede line; DIS's heading lede line; the lede 6-line budget; the standing ABNB/CART/ARCC/MCO/AFRM/LIN/EOG/SKYD/COIN/pending-filing/Wikipedia/batch-121/CCI/STZ items and the batch-157 gate skips.

## 2026-11-02 — Batch 178: exposed table-row/title leak predicates (COF/ARCC/EL)

**What:** Fifteen extractor changes (`data/filings.py`) plus a repair run (`scripts/batch178_repair.py`, 13/13 rows repaired): the `$-figures` cap 80→120 in a narrowed 81–120 band with four guards (no bare-year ending, no `$` figure + scale word, no `$-to-$` range, no flattened-list bullet); spaced-decimal `$` counting; new `_is_lede_title_line` (trailing heading, no headline verb) as a lede-loop skip; `_is_lede_asof_date`; table-header footnote-digit stripping + "Earnings" metric; singular-"dollar" units regex; colon bound 8→12 words; headline-verb additions (AFFIRMS/PROVIDES/CONFIRMS/REVISES/INITIATES/REITERATES/REITERATING); `_LEDE_BARE_UNITS_RE` + lowercase form + "Dollars in" form; glued footnote numbers; footnote-definition blocks crossing blank lines and bare page numbers; and a trailing bare-units trim in `_clean_highlights_result`. Repair: ARCC ×5, COF ×7, EL ×1 — 13 rows, live==blast verified, DB-verified.

**Why:** The batch-177 repair exposed the leak: freeing lede budget lets the loop reach lines the old extraction never served. Ares Capital served an 86-char recon row, a units line, a bare "As of December 31," fragment and a 10-word yields label; Capital One served its own exhibit title plus a Walmart recon row; Estée Lauder served spaced-decimal "$ .44 $ .91" rows and a Non-GAAP header. Each line consumed one of the 6 lede lines and starved a real bullet (COF lost its revenue/expense bullets; EL lost its margin bullets; ARCC's stored rows were skeletons). The probe showed the class is DB-wide (the blast moved 133 quarters), so the fix is predicates, not per-row edits.

**Design notes:** The 120-char band is deliberately narrow and guarded, not a blunt raise: a blunt 80→120 broke 6 tests on the first attempt (COIN Q1/Q2 wrapped prose, Chubb bullet continuations, the batch-160 comparison clause, a bare-number line ending in a word, Eaton's year-ending line, Amex/Cencora `$-to-$` ranges) — each guard is one of those failures, and the ≤80 branch is byte-identical. The title-line verb veto is load-bearing in the other direction: without it, "Q2 ORGANIC SALES AND ADJUSTED EPS EXCEED OUTLOOK" and "COMPANY MAINTAINS FULL-YEAR REVENUE AND EPS GUIDANCE" would drop. The footnote-block fix has two halves that look alike but are different bugs: a blank line inside an unfinished definition (the block must consume it, but a terminated definition still stops at blanks — the test pins both), and a bare page number ("2") inside a wrapped definition (an admin line, so `_is_block_boundary` fires — the block must treat a lone 1–3 digit line as transparent, because a lone bare number is never a footnote key). The bare-units regex has three forms for a reason: the capital form keeps the `(data|information)` tail so a prose sentence that merely mentions units ("In billions, ... revenue grew 8%.") survives; the lowercase form is tail-free because a sentence never opens lowercase; the "Dollars in" form is tail-free because its opening is itself the units signature. The final-cleanup trim exists because a Highlights section serves its lines directly — eBay's units fragment never passes the lede loop's skip, so only the post-assembly trim can catch it; the 200-char floor keeps the trim from dropping a short row to None (the test pins a sub-200 body staying put).

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, baseline `/tmp/blast178c/old`, final `/tmp/blast178c/new5`): 133 diffs, 1,566 byte-identical, 0 true content losses (every removed line is a defect line; the freed budget serves real bullets/quotes/paras instead). Round 1 (`/tmp/blast178c/new`, 134 diffs) had 6 regressions, all fixed by the round-2 predicates; round 2 (`/tmp/blast178c/new2`, 128 diffs) added the intended glued-footnote drops; the final +5 are the post-fix scan's bare-units residuals. A post-fix residual scan of the final blast finds 0 as-of dates, 0 parenthesised units lines, 0 standalone bare-units lines, 0 spaced-decimal rows. 18 new tests; full suite 1314 green. The pin is behavioral: the attested ARCC/COF/EL lines fail the predicates on stashed old code, and the footnote-block test fails on the pre-fix code (block returns 1, expected 4).

**Related:** Closes the exposed table-row/title leak (ARCC recon rows, COF title lines, EL spaced-decimal rows, ARCC as-of/yields lines), the COF ×7 batch-177 repair remainder, the glued-footnote class and the bare-units declaration class. Still open: the batch-177 repair remainder minus COF (19 rows — GEV ×8, CMG ×5, AIG ×2, ADBE, APD, CHD, CPB); the new blast washes (V ×7 "% change" footnote flattened onto a quote attribution; EMR residue swap + investor-conference notice; FICO "GAAP Revenue" headings; GPC segment-label lines; CSX header+value line; CEG reconciliation intro); the 500-char cap's two survivors; the lede 6-line budget; the batch-176 31-row audit remainder; AFRM's page-header-prefix false positive; the standing ABNB/CART/ARCC-table-section-tail/MCO/AFRM/LIN/EOG/SKYD/COIN/pending-filing/Wikipedia/batch-121/CCI/STZ items and the batch-157 gate skips.

## 2026-11-02 — Batch 179: PR/IR contact labels + batch-177 remainder repair (28 rows)

**What:** Two extractor changes (`data/filings.py`) plus a repair run (`scripts/batch179_repair.py`, 28/29 rows repaired): `_CONTACT_LINE_RE` gains a word-boundaried `\b(pr|ir)[\s\-–]*(contacts?|inquiries?)\b` branch, and `_CONTACT_HEADER_RE` gains `pr|ir` in its role-prefix list. Repair: GEV ×8, CMG ×7, AIG ×8, CPB ×3, APD ×1, CHD ×1 — 28 rows, live==blast verified, DB-verified. 3 new tests; full suite 1317 green.

**Why:** Chipotle heads every release with a two-block contact head (PR Contact + phone + email, IR Contact + phone + email), and the contact regexes only knew the spelled-out roles — so the headline loop picked "PR Contact: Laurie Schalow" as the headline and every CMG stored row opens with it, while the IR line was dropped inconsistently (the old extraction kept PR, dropped IR). AIG's rows open with the "www.aig.com Quentin McMillan (Investors): ..." contact lines, which the repair gates didn't recognise as contact-block lines. The classes are per-ticker (every CMG/AIG quarter), so the repair cohort is all 8 DB quarters of each, not just the 5+2 remainder rows — the blast attests the fixed extraction for all 12 CMG cache quarters.

**Design notes:** The `\b` before `(pr|ir)` is load-bearing: without it "their contact" and "first contact" would match (the "ir" sits mid-word there). The header change matters more than the line change for the headline loop: the header branch fires first and `_skip_contact_block` consumes the phone/email detail lines, while the line branch alone would skip only the header line and leave the phone/email lines for other branches to (maybe) catch — traced on the verbatim CMG head, the block skip stops exactly at the real headline. The repair gates took three rounds: (1) the lede-summary exclusion as "unterminated figure-free line before a colon heading" matched AIG's real headline (it sits before "Fourth Quarter 2024:") — the fix is positional, a summary is the paragraph right after the first real paragraph, and the first-real index is computed with the summary-free defect set to avoid circularity; (2) the wrapped-headline heuristic (fragment half + year in the join) falsely exempted CHD's "UPDATES FULL YEAR OUTLOOK", whose neighbour merely happened to be the year-carrying section title "2025 First Quarter Results" — the fix is an exact anchor, a stored paragraph is a headline half iff it joins with a neighbour into the live headline, threaded through `_first_real_index` / `_stored_real_headline` / the preservation gates (which is why the main loop now fetches live before looking up the stored headline); the join guard itself simplified to exact equality, since an exact join IS the evidence the next paragraph is headline material (round 2's defect check on it wrongly blocked CMG 2025-12-31's second half, a title line by the extractor's own predicate); (3) the "■" bullet marker is stripped in the repair script's `_core_key` (AIG bullets are "■ " stored vs "■" live) and the residue scan skips "■"/"▪"/"○"-led lines as bullets — `_is_lede_data_row` had flagged AIG's net-income bullets as data rows because `_BULLET_LEAD_RE` doesn't match "■". The CMG 2025-06-30 skip is a genuine extractor question, not a gate artifact: the all-caps sub-headline's June-positive-comps fact appears nowhere in live, so no gate may drop it.

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (`scripts/batch179_cache_blast.py`, baseline `/tmp/blast179c/old`, new `/tmp/blast179c/new`): 12 diffs, 1,687 byte-identical, 0 true content losses — all CMG; 9 quarters drop the contact paragraph and gain one previously-starved bullet, 3 quarters change only line 1 from the contact line to the real headline. 3 new tests; full suite 1317 green. The pin is behavioral: on stashed old code the regex test and the CMG end-to-end test fail (the prose-negative test passes on both, as designed).

**Related:** Closes the batch-177 repair remainder except ADBE and CMG 2025-06-30 (28 of 29 rows), the PR/IR contact-label class, the GEV lede-summary sub-headline class and the CPB wrapped-headline class. Still open: CMG 2025-06-30's all-caps sub-headline (live drops it; June-positive-comps fact unrepeated); ADBE 2025-11-28's section-pick judgment (FY bullets superseded by same-metric Q4 bullets + Disclosure/Targets boilerplate exclusions); the batch-178 blast washes (V ×7, EMR, FICO, GPC, CSX, CEG); the 500-char cap's two survivors; the lede 6-line budget; the batch-176 31-row audit remainder; AFRM's page-header-prefix false positive; the standing ABNB/CART/ARCC-table-section-tail/MCO/AFRM/LIN/EOG/SKYD/COIN/pending-filing/Wikipedia/batch-121/CCI/STZ items and the batch-157 gate skips.

## 2026-11-02 — Batch 180: "% change" footnote glued onto a quote attribution (Visa)

**What:** Three extractor changes (`data/filings.py`) plus a repair run (`scripts/batch180_repair.py`, 1/8 rows repaired): (1) `_is_lede_footnote_attribution` — a line carrying both a "% change is calculated" footnote fragment and a quote-attribution tail is table residue, wired into `_is_lede_table_line` before the termination gate; (2) `_is_lede_colon_heading`'s quote-attribution veto gains commented/comments; (3) the lede finish rule treats a quote attribution on the budget line as unfinished, so it still pulls its quote in. Repair: V 2024-06-30 (449→871), live==blast verified, DB-verified. 5 new tests; full suite 1322 green.

**Why:** Visa flattens its summary-table footnote, the table's period header and the CEO-quote attribution onto one lede line ("% change is calculated over the comparable prior-year period. Q3 2024 Ryan McInerney, Chief Executive Officer, Visa, commented on the results:"), served in 7 non-Q4 quarters. It is a batch-178 regression: the freed lede budget (the bare-units line above it is now skipped) lets the loop reach it, while the stored rows predate that and are clean — live was worse than stored. The footnote and the period header are the table's own text, and the attribution without its quote is a dangling fragment; dropping the whole line frees the budget for the quote itself, which follows the table interleaved with the data rows.

**Design notes:** Both halves of the predicate are required, and each half alone is already handled elsewhere: a bare "% change is calculated ..." line is a footnote-definition block, and a bare attribution is a legitimate quote intro (the colon-heading veto protects "... said:" lines) — so the predicate can only fire on the glued shape. The veto extension was found through the test, not the blast: the synthetic bare-attribution release served the quote but dropped the attribution, and the probe showed `_is_lede_colon_heading`'s veto covers said/says only (noted/added are vetoed via `_LEDE_COLON_VERB_RE`; commented was in neither). The finish rule needed two coordinated touches for the same reason: the attribution at the budget is neither a wrap tail (it ends in ":") nor sentence-unterminated (`_is_sentence_terminated` counts ":" as terminal, by design for the table-line gate) — so the finish-item decision gains an attribution check and the finish-sentence break gains an attribution suppression. The broad alternative was tried and reverted in the same batch: treating ANY colon-ended budget item as unfinished pulled Berkshire's table-label lines in (BRK.B ×7 "Net earnings includes:" plus AKAM/COF/FDX/BSX/FAST/GILD — 27 extra diffs, 34 total), so the colon is only a continuation signal when it is a quote attribution. The reverted round is preserved in the blast dirs (`/tmp/blast180c/new3`, 34 diffs) against the final (`/tmp/blast180c/new4`, 7 diffs).

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, baseline `/tmp/blast180c/old`, final `/tmp/blast180c/new4`): 7 diffs, 1,692 byte-identical, 0 true content losses — each V quarter swaps the defect line 1-for-1 for the CEO quote; a scan of the final blast finds 0 "% change is calculated" lines. 5 new tests; full suite 1322 green. The pin is behavioral: on stashed old code the test file errors at collection (the predicate does not exist).

**Related:** Closes the V "% change" footnote-attribution class (7 quarters) from the batch-178 blast washes, the "commented" colon-heading veto gap, and quote-attribution-at-budget orphaning. Still open: V Q4 rows (2024-09-30, 2025-09-30) still carry the defect line in the DB — the gates refuse the repair because live drops the quote + repurchases/dividend bullets at the 6-line budget; V prose rows ×5 (stored ~4000-char prose ledes vs live tight bullets + quote) — condensation is a product judgment; CMG 2025-06-30's all-caps sub-headline; ADBE 2025-11-28's section-pick judgment; the batch-178 blast washes minus V (EMR, FICO, GPC, CSX, CEG); the 500-char cap's two survivors; the lede 6-line budget; the batch-176 31-row audit remainder; AFRM's page-header-prefix false positive; the standing ABNB/CART/ARCC-table-section-tail/MCO/AFRM/LIN/EOG/SKYD/COIN/pending-filing/Wikipedia/batch-121/CCI/STZ items and the batch-157 gate skips.

## 2026-11-02 — Batch 181: Emerson lede residue swap + FICO "GAAP Revenue" headings

**What:** Eight extractor changes (`data/filings.py`) plus repair runs (21/33 blast-diff quarters repaired: 13 EMR/FICO via `scripts/batch181_repair.py` + 8 APP/CSX/MCO/XOM via `scripts/batch181_repair_rest.py`): (1) `_is_table_intro_only` — the table-intro branch only fires when the intro sentence IS the line, restoring Emerson's guidance paragraph; (2) `_is_lede_single_value_row` — single-value guidance/balance-sheet table rows ("Operating Cash Flow ~$3.6B", "IEEPA tariff refunds - ~($0.11)"); (3) `_is_lede_split_quote_attribution` + `_lede_quote_is_open` — a split quote attribution no longer orphans the quote's second half; (4) `_INVESTOR_CONFERENCE_NOTICE_RE` + `_is_lede_conf_call_heading` — investor-conference notices and the "Conference Call and Upcoming Events" heading; (5) table-header gaps — EBITA/EBIT in the metric regex, 3-word floor, bare-year transparency ("Adjusted Segment EBITA4", FICO's "GAAP Revenue"); (6) the single-value predicate's last-word guard extends "to" → {"to", "of"}; (7) `_BARE_CELL_RE` gains a lone $ amount ("$ 0.44"); (8) `_is_bare_numbered_footnote` cap 500 → 1000. Repair: EMR ×5 (2024-09-30, 2025-03-31, 2025-06-30, 2025-09-30, 2026-06-30) + FICO ×8, live==blast verified, DB-verified. 14 new tests; full suite 1336 green.

**Why:** Batch 178 freed lede budget by dropping table residue, and the loop then reached deeper into Emerson's releases — where it served guidance-table rows and an investor-conference notice, dropped the guidance paragraph the table-intro rule over-matched, and orphaned a CEO quote at a split attribution; live was worse than stored on four EMR quarters. The same wash exposed FICO's "GAAP Revenue" table header (served in 10 quarters, with the revenue paragraph + CEO quote ungained behind it) and CSX's recon-table EPS cell. Fixes 6–8 came from the first blast's own review (41 diffs): the new predicate over-fired on "of"-ended summary lines (BBY/C/DUK) and under-fired on the lone $ cell (CSX) and long footnotes (EMR 699-char Copeland footnote) the freed budget exposed.

**Design notes:** The "of" guard is the shape that separates a table row from a wrapped summary line: a table-row label ends in a noun, never a preposition — measured over the cache, 140 single-value candidates end in "of" and every one sampled is a prose summary line ("BOOK VALUE PER SHARE OF $99.08", "reported net income of $374.2", "Board of Directors Declares Quarterly Cash Dividend of $0.59"), while "at"/"for" have zero attested candidates and stay out of the guard. The lone-$ cell is safe because a metric value carries a scale word or suffix ("$50.2 billion", "$1.2B"), which the number-only class never matches — the batch-157 lone-value rule is untouched. The cap raise is evidence-backed, not a guess: the 500–1000 band is footnotes throughout (~190 attested lines, every one sampled a definition), and above 1000 the population is dominated by page/table mega-lines (Ford's 3000-char flattened highlights table, BofA's balance-sheet table) with only a few real footnotes interspersed, so the cap stays at 1000 and those longer ones survive as tails. The "GAAP Results" heading is deliberately NOT dropped by the table-header predicate ("Results" is not a metric word) — it is a title line, which is what the repair gates need, and whether the first-section heading should drop is a separate judgment. One accepted trade: EMR 2024-06-30 (cache-only, no DB row) serves the "Copeland Transaction Update" section heading + its prose paragraph once the footnote drops — the heading is the title of the paragraph it introduces, so the pair reads coherently; a generic section-heading predicate would be needed to drop it.

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, baseline regenerated via `git stash` into the persistent `hidden_files/blast181c/base`, final `hidden_files/blast181c/new` — `/tmp` was wiped mid-batch, so the blast dirs now live under `hidden_files`): 33 diffs, 1,666 byte-identical, 0 true content losses — the first blast's 41 diffs minus BBY/C/DUK (15, reverted by the "of" guard) plus 8 new from the cap raise and lone-$ cell (AXP, DASH, MCO, XOM ×4, FICO 2026 quarters); every removed line is table residue, a footnote definition, a notice, or a table header, and every added line is real prose, a quote, or a bullet. Every one of the 33 diffs was inspected. 14 new tests; full suite 1336 green. The pin is behavioral: on stashed old code the test file errors at collection (the predicates do not exist).

**Rest repair (same batch):** `scripts/batch181_repair_rest.py`, same gates, 10 targets (APP/AXP/CSX/DASH/MCO/XOM): 8 repaired — APP 2024-09-30 (876→539), CSX 2025-09-30 (926→1370), MCO 2024-12-31 (2119→1613) and 2025-03-31 (1349→1393), XOM ×4 (2312→1722, 2639→2048, 2612→2015, 2997→2434); 2 skipped gate-correct — AXP 2025-06-30 (live still carries the pre-existing conference-call notice; the stored row shares it, so the gate rightly refuses to bless the row) and DASH 2025-06-30 (live still carries the pre-existing "Financial Performance" mid-letter heading). Both are carried follow-ups: the AXP notice needs its collection path found (the batch-166 regex matches the text, so the notice is served through a path the regex branch never sees), and mid-letter section headings need their own mechanism. Batch-181 repair total: 21 rows of the 23 with stored highlights; the other 10 blast-diff quarters have no stored highlights row (seeding them is a separate job).

**Related:** Closes the Emerson lede residue swap, the FICO "GAAP Revenue" headings (10 quarters), the CSX "$ 0.44" lone-value residue, and the 500-char footnote cap's two survivors (MCO 556-char disclaimer, AXP 671-char FX footnote) from the batch-178 blast washes. Still open: V Q4 rows and prose rows ×5; CMG 2025-06-30's all-caps sub-headline; ADBE 2025-11-28's section-pick judgment; the batch-178 blast washes minus V/EMR/FICO/CSX (GPC, CEG); the lede 6-line budget (the DUK CEO quotes and Citi prose paragraphs the "of" guard trades away are budget-bound); the batch-176 31-row audit remainder; AFRM's page-header-prefix false positive; the >1000-char footnotes (Nike 1071, Ford 1179, Coinbase 1262); EMR 2024-06-30's "Copeland Transaction Update" section heading; the standing ABNB/CART/ARCC-table-section-tail/MCO/AFRM/LIN/EOG/SKYD/COIN/pending-filing/Wikipedia/batch-121/CCI/STZ items and the batch-157 gate skips.

## 2026-11-03 — Batch 182: batch-178 blast washes (GPC segment heading, CEG recon intro, CSX header+value line) + BRO recon heading and AXP/EQIX density fixes

**What:** Twelve extractor changes (`data/filings.py`) plus a repair run (7/12 targets repaired): (1) `_is_lede_recon_heading` — a 2–10-word, capital-led, unterminated, figure-free line carrying "reconcil" with no headline verb (CEG ×5, BRO ×7, CSX); (2) `_is_recon_heading_wrap` — the lowercase-led ≤6-word wrap tail ("to Organic Revenue"), consulted only immediately after a skipped recon heading; (3) `_is_lede_segment_heading` — ends `Group ("<alias>")`, 3–10 words, capital-led, unterminated, figure-free (GPC); (4) `_LEDE_BARE_UNITS_DOLLARS_RE` (a line that IS just "Dollars in millions,") + `_is_lede_split_units_continuation` ("except per share amounts" + a table metric word), both wired into `_is_lede_table_line` before the termination gate; (5) `_is_lede_call_announcement` ("will conduct/host/hold … conference call" + a time or "dial") + `_is_lede_webcast_continuation` ("In conjunction with the call … webcast", only after a skipped notice) — CSX's split notice (6 quarters); (6) `_is_lede_report_pointer` — "This earnings announcement / press release / earnings release" + "is contained in" / "should be read in conjunction with" (CSX ×12, Amex ×12), plus the "See information regarding … presented later in this press release" form (BRO ×12); (7) `_FOOTNOTE_POINTER_RE` takes the space after the number as optional — glued pointers ("1See…", "1Refer to…"); (8) `_is_lede_table_header` skips pure-punctuation dash tokens ("Diluted Net Income Per Share - Adjusted"); (9) `_RULE_LINE_RE` gains a hash-separator alternative ("# # #", Amex's flattened "###" tail, 5 quarters). Repair: CSX ×4 + AXP ×3, live==blast verified, DB-verified. 18 new tests; full suite 1354 green.

**Why:** The batch-178 blast left three washes (GPC segment-label lines, CEG reconciliation intro, CSX header+value line), and batch 181's rest repair left the AXP notice follow-up open (the batch-166 regex matches the notice text, so it was served through a path the regex branch never sees — the section collector, which had no notice handling). All four are one cluster: a reconciliation table's heading and boilerplate intro, a business-segment heading, a split units declaration, and a split conference-call notice leak into highlights as lede lines or section content. Fixes 8–9 came from the batch-182 blast's own diffs: freeing the budget past BRO's recon table exposed its "See information" pointer, its dash-joined adjusted-EPS header, and Amex's "# # #" separator tail.

**Design notes:** The recon heading is a BREAK for Highlights-section collection but only a SKIP in the lede loop — CSX's CEO quote and volume paragraph follow its recon table in document order, and a break would lose them; the section path has no such following content, so the break is safe there. The density mechanism is the same at CEG and EQIX: the recon intro's tax percentages (CEG) and the notice tail (EQIX) inflate the wrong section's financial density past the batch-102 0.4 bar, so the operations section (CEG) / generic leading bullets (EQIX) win the ranking; breaking collection at the heading / notice restores the tight section (CEG: Highlights bullets + dateline + quotes, the shape 2025-03-31 already had; EQIX: 7 Business Highlights bullets, 3/7=0.43). The EQIX trade is accepted and named: the guidance-raise bullet + MRR/stabilized-assets figures are lost from highlights and live on the detail page. The report-pointer opener is load-bearing: Charter's "The information in this press release should be read in conjunction with …" is mid-paragraph boilerplate, not a line-opener, so it never qualifies. The "See information" form needs its "presented later in this press release" tail — a bare "See information regarding X" without it could be a real cross-reference sentence. The dash skip is safe because the metric-word guard still applies: AJG's "Summary of Financial Results - Third Quarter" and the ELV dateline both carry " - " and stay out. The hash rule is safe because a line of nothing but "#" is a separator, never content.

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, `hidden_files/blast182c/base` → `new6`): 27 diffs, 1,672 byte-identical, 0 true content losses — BRO ×7, CEG ×6, CSX ×4, GPC ×2, AXP ×5, EQIX ×1; every diff inspected. The residual scan over all 1,699 outputs is zero for every batch-182 defect class; the remaining hits are inside real prose/headline sentences (APLD's quoted "Reconciliation of GAAP to Non-GAAP Measures", BRO's headline "Diluted Net Income Per Share - Adjusted of $1.14") or pre-existing and unchanged (GPC 2025-12-31's lone "North America Automotive" alias fragment, DD 2023-09-30's "Dollars in millions, unless noted 3Q'23 3Q'22 Change", AXP 2024's "(Millions, except per share amounts, and where indicated)" units titles). One accepted non-repair: GPC 2026-03-31's live now serves the segment paragraph instead of the dangling heading, but the stored row (2,966 chars) is a prose dump — a section-pick judgment, not repaired, same precedent as V/ADBE.

**Repair (same batch):** `scripts/batch182_repair.py`, batch-181 gates + the six batch-182 predicates as defect exclusions and live-residue checks, 12 targets (CEG ×4, CSX ×4, AXP ×3, EQIX ×1): 7 repaired — CSX 2025-03-31 (2164→1261), 2025-06-30 (1582→685), 2025-09-30 (1370→1621), 2026-03-31 (2145→1259), AXP 2025-06-30 (3084→792), 2025-09-30 (2402→2026), 2026-03-31 (2420→1967); 5 skipped gate-correct — CEG ×4 (section-pick refusals: the stored operations-section bullets are real content the tighter Highlights section drops) and EQIX 2026-03-31 (headline mismatch: the stored headline is truncated to "Equinix Reports First-Quarter Results" while live has the full "…and Raises Full-Year Financial Outlook", a pre-existing stored-row staleness, plus a stored-only "Investor Presentation and Supplemental Financial Information" heading line). One repair-script-only norm fix (batch-170 precedent): `norm()` now attaches ™/®/© — the stored AXP 2026-03-31 bullet flattens "Experiences ™" (spaced) while live attaches it, tripping the 60-char preservation check on exactly one character. BRO ×5 was deliberately excluded from the targets: the batch-182 live still ends with two orphaned table-row label lines ("Diluted net income per share", "Change in estimated acquisition earn-out payables") whose $-value rows the fragment-run dropper already ate, so no gate predicate sees them and a repair would write known residue into the DB.

**Related:** Closes the batch-178 GPC/CEG/CSX washes; the batch-181 AXP notice follow-up; BRO's recon heading + wrap; the Amex "# # #" separator tail; the CSX split notice/pointer/glued-footnote class. ## 2026-11-03 — Batch 183: GPC $-to-$ range sub-headline dropped by lede data-row rule; 29-row repair + repair-gate hardening

**What:** One extractor fix (`data/filings.py`, 56 insertions) plus a repair run (29/51 targets repaired): (1) `_is_lede_dollar_range_statement` + `_LEDE_DOLLAR_RANGE_RE` — a `$-to-$` range carried by ≥4 alphabetic words besides "to" is a guidance STATEMENT, never a table row — hoisted to the top of `_is_lede_data_row`, so the ≤80-char branch and the ≥40% density branch no longer eat short guidance sub-headlines (the batch-178 guard only covered the 81–120 band); the 81–120 band's inline range check now reuses the shared regex. (2) Lede-loop free-append: a range statement served in the pre-body zone (no terminated line collected yet) is appended WITHOUT consuming a budget line. 7 new tests; full suite 1361 green.

**Why:** GPC 2026-06-30's "Reaffirms 2026 Outlook for Adjusted EPS of $7.50 to $8.00" (55 chars) was dropped by the ≤80-char data-row branch — a guidance sub-headline misclassified as a table row. The first attempt (predicate-only) produced 23 blast diffs but with bad trades — AXP lost its results dateline paragraph, BBY lost its CEO quote, GPC lost its adjusted-net-income paragraph (the 6-line budget) — and MCO gained a dangling "$10.40 to $11.00" line. The refined fix serves the sub-headline without starving real content: the free-append in the pre-body zone plus the ≥4-word statement bar.

**Design notes:** The ≥4-word bar is what keeps Moody's "$10.40 to $11.00" (bare fragment, 0 words) and the dense "EPS $7.50 to $8.00" (1 word) as data rows — a range statement needs a sentence around it, not just a label. The free-append only fires pre-body (no terminated line yet), so it cannot inflate mid-body content; the past-dateline bound still applies. The 81–120 band reuse keeps one regex as the single source of truth for "is this a $-to-$ range".

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, `hidden_files/blast183c/base` → `new2`): 51 diffs, 1,648 byte-identical, 0 content losses — every old paragraph verified as a subsequence of new. Diff tickers: ABT ×3, AEP ×1, APD ×4, AWK ×10, AXP ×2, BBY ×10, BMY ×7, COR ×9, DUK ×1, ED ×1, EIX ×1, GPC ×2; every diff a pure addition (range sub-headline, or a real bullet/quote/dateline via the freed budget slot). MCO byte-identical to baseline.

**Repair (same batch):** `scripts/batch183_repair.py` (batch-181 gates + batch-183 predicates as defect exclusions and live-residue checks), 51 targets: 29 repaired — ABT 2025-12-31 (789→933), 2026-03-31 (775→917); APD 2025-06-30 (557→1617), 2025-12-31 (726→865), 2026-06-30 (605→1261); AWK 2024-09-30 (786→1004), 2025-03-31 (671→1063), 2025-06-30 (414→1298), 2025-12-31 (855→930), 2026-03-31 (715→942), 2026-06-30 (910→1083), 2026-09-30 (819→1116); AXP 2024-12-31 (419→663); BBY 2025-02-01 (511→880), 2025-05-03 (476→1226), 2025-08-02 (449→1729), 2025-11-01 (450→1692), 2026-05-02 (452→989), 2026-08-01 (498→965); BMY 2025-03-31 (577→1071), 2025-06-30 (779→1327), 2025-09-30 (814→1222), 2025-12-31 (789→897), 2026-03-31 (801→1276), 2026-06-30 (620→1087), 2026-09-30 (788→913); DUK 2025-09-30 (1464→1941); GPC 2026-06-30 (1024→1812), 2026-09-30 (2066→2143). 22 skipped gate-correct: 14 rows with no stored highlights row; COR ×6 (section-pick refusals — live drops stored bullets like the RCA acquisition / Good Neighbor Pharmacy bullets); BBY 2026-01-31 (live drops stored "Revenue ($ in millions)" line). Three repair-script-only gate changes: range-statement exemptions in the script-local `$` guards; `_is_trailing_heading` position-aware via a sub-headline zone prefix (`_zone_end` walks consecutive sub-headline lines after the headline — `_is_subheadline_zone_line`: ≤10 words, unterminated, capital-led or small-word/number, never bullet-led, figures allowed — or non-bullet range statements; a repeated headline still blocks); "non-GAAP financial measure" marker narrowed to the definition shape (`is/are a non-GAAP financial measure(s)`). Gate verification: old-vs-new scan over all 1,699 outputs, iterated 5 versions (195 → 100 → 90 → 93 → 93 files changed, 0 newly flagged in the final version); every unflagged line reviewed and classified as genuine guidance statements, pre-body sub-headlines, or real non-GAAP prose; section headings, repeated headlines, and exhibit titles still block. The one known borderline: APD 2026-03-31's "* Certain results in this release include references to non-GAAP financial measures…" asterisk-note line is no longer flagged.

**Related:** Closes the GPC $-to-$ range class (batch-182's open item). ## 2026-11-03 — Batch 184: CMG all-caps sub-headline served as section content; 2 stored rows repaired

**What:** Three extractor changes (`data/filings.py`) plus a repair run (2/2 rows repaired): (1) `_HIGHLIGHTS_HEADING_RE`'s leading branch narrows from bare `^highlights?\b` to heading-shaped tails (`of|from|for|include` + unterminated rest); (2) `_is_lede_heading_fragment` gains a leading-"Highlights" carve-out — an all-caps line starting with "Highlights" that is not a highlights heading per the regex is a sub-headline, not a ≤4-word heading fragment; (3) the section collector splits the regex's two duties — `_HIGHLIGHTS_SECTION_ANCHOR_RE` keeps the broad pattern as the section-start signal, a sub-headline anchor is prepended as the section's first content line, and the 12-line cap counts follow lines only.

**Why:** Chipotle's all-caps sub-headlines sit between the headline and the dateline and carry real news — "HIGHLIGHTS RETURN TO POSITIVE COMPARABLE SALES AND TRANSACTIONS IN JUNE" (Q2 2025: June comps turned positive while the quarter was down 4.0%) — but the bare `^highlights?\b` branch matched any line starting with the word, so the lede loop, the trailing trim, the block boundary and the section collector all treated them as section headings and dropped them. The 2025-06-30 stored row kept the sub-headline (extracted with older code) while live dropped it, which is why the batch-179 repair skipped it as "a genuine extractor question, not a gate artifact".

**Design notes:** The narrowed rule is measured, not guessed: a full-cache survey of every `^highlights` line (17 distinct) shows the real heading shapes after a leading "Highlights" are exactly bare, "OF SEGMENT RESULTS", "FROM THE QUARTER/YEAR", "from the report include:", "for the … are listed below:" — and the terminator guard excludes Delta's pointer sentence while the tail-word guard excludes Airbnb's flattened-prose fragment. "RETURN" stays out of `_LEDE_HEADLINE_VERB_RE` deliberately: "Return on Equity" / "Return on Invested Capital" table labels would escape the table-header predicate. The anchor split was forced by the first blast: narrowing the regex alone collapsed both CMG sections to the 6-line lede (bullets 5–7, quote, prose lost) and flipped DAL to the "Operations, Network and Fleet" section — because the sub-headline and the DAL pointer sentence were the anchors of the sections serving the real content. The prepend is gated on all-caps + unterminated so the terminated DAL pointer stays a heading-only anchor (its quotes still serve, the pointer itself never does). The follow-line-only cap is what keeps the prepend from costing the trailing revenue prose: the old cap counted the anchor, so the 12th follow line ("Total revenue in the second quarter of 2025 was $3.1 billion…") fell off; sections without a prepended anchor are byte-identical by construction (n_follow == len(collected)).

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, `hidden_files/blast184c/base` → `new`): 2 diffs, 1,697 byte-identical, 0 true content losses — each diff a pure one-line addition of the CMG sub-headline; DAL byte-identical. The intermediate blasts are preserved in the reasoning: blast 1 (regex only) 3 diffs with 2 real losses (CMG lede truncation, DAL section flip); blast 2 (anchor + prepend, old cap) 2 diffs but each quarter's trailing revenue prose dropped to the cap. 6 new tests; full suite 1367 green. The pin is behavioral: on stashed old code the test file errors at collection (the regex/anchor names do not exist).

**Repair (same batch):** `scripts/batch184_repair.py` (batch-183 gates, no gate change needed — the served sub-headline carries no $ or % and ends in "JUNE"/"SALES", not a heading end-word, so no residue guard flags it), 2 targets: CMG 2025-06-30 (427→1748 — also a truncation repair: the stored row held only contact lines, headline, sub-headline, dateline, intro and one bullet from a pre-batch-179 extraction) and CMG 2025-09-30 (1553→1590), live==blast verified, DB-verified.

**Related:** Closes the CMG all-caps sub-headline class (batch-179's open item, carried since batch 177). Still open: COR ×6 section-pick refusals; BBY 2026-01-31 "Revenue ($ in millions)" stored line; APD 2026-03-31 asterisk-note line in the section path; V Q4 rows and prose rows ×5; ADBE 2025-11-28's section-pick judgment; GPC 2026-03-31's prose-dump stored row; the BRO orphaned label lines (needs a combined fix); EQIX 2026-03-31's truncated stored headline; the AXP 2024 units-title lines and the DD "Dollars in millions, unless noted" line; the GPC 2025-12-31 alias fragment; the CSX dateline ".1" glue; the lede 6-line budget; the batch-176 31-row audit remainder; AFRM's page-header-prefix false positive; the >1000-char footnotes (Nike 1071, Ford 1179, Coinbase 1262); EMR 2024-06-30's "Copeland Transaction Update" section heading; the standing ABNB/CART/ARCC-table-section-tail/MCO/AFRM/LIN/EOG/SKYD/COIN/pending-filing/Wikipedia/batch-121/CCI/STZ items and the batch-157 gate skips.

## 2026-11-03 — Batch 185: BRO orphaned flattened-table labels dropped; EOG supplement packet stays None; 5 stored rows repaired

**What:** Table mode for the lede loop (`data/filings.py`) plus a repair run (5/7 rows repaired, 2 skipped gate-correct): (1) `_is_lede_flat_table_label` — a flattened table row's label below a table header block is skipped and enters table mode; (2) `_is_lede_table_mode_residue` — unterminated / colon-ended / "Note:" / corporate-suffix company-name / "respectively."-with-year lines are table residue in mode, terminated prose and bullets still end it; (3) `_is_lede_nongaap_section_heading` — the bare "Non-GAAP Financial Measures" heading breaks the loop in table mode only, placed at the top of the loop body; (4) the `table_seen` gate — prose reached in mode with no content collected and no real table row seen breaks instead of collecting (a TOC-only packet stays None); lone "—" joins `_is_lone_bullet_marker`.

**Why:** Brown & Brown flattens recon tables one cell per line. The label's `$-value` rows drop as a fragment run, so "Diluted net income per share" and "Change in estimated acquisition earn-out payables" survived as the last two lede lines in 7 quarters (2024-03-31 through 2025-09-30). The label is too long for the heading fold (4-word cap) and too lowercase-led for `_is_lede_table_header`, and the batch-160 table-label predicate could not see it because its next line is the next label, not a data row. The labels filled the last 2 slots of the 6-line lede budget, starving the AXP dateline + CEO quotes the same way (AXP ×2 gains in the blast).

**Design notes:** v1 broke on the flat label and mistook wrapped sub-headlines for label runs (AAPL/AXP/CCI/COF/CSX/EL/FDX, 45 diffs, 21 bad) — the loop must skip-and-continue, not break, because narrative can follow a table, and the label-run prev branch had to go. v2 (skip + mode) got to 15 diffs but leaked lone "—" cells (BRO ×5) and served EOG's non-GAAP definitions (×4). The "—" fix alone did not save BRO 2024-03-31: once the labels stopped filling the budget, the loop walked past the narrative into the balance sheet and collected a "respectively."-flattened row plus the cash-flow title and rows via the overrun — the "respectively."-with-year residue rule fixed it in v4. The non-GAAP break had to move to the top of the loop body: `_is_lede_title_line` also matches the bare heading and skips it before a later break can run. The `table_seen` gate was forced by a full-suite regression: table mode let the loop skip the TOC entries of the batch-126 synthetic and reach the filler prose, turning an honest None into served lorem — the gate distinguishes "filler after a TOC" (residue only, no table row seen, no content yet) from "narrative after a table" (data rows / unit lines / period headers seen, or content already collected). The EOG synthetic test had to mirror the real packet (exhibit headers, so "Table of Contents" is the headline; a >140-char footnote line, so `_skip_toc` returns unchanged with pages=1) before it exercised the real path.

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, `hidden_files/blast185c/base` → `new`): 11 diffs, 1,688 byte-identical, 0 content losses — AXP ×2 (labels dropped, dateline + CEO quotes/guidance now served), BRO ×7 (only the two labels removed, narrative kept), CCI ×1 ("Change % Change" dropped, CEO quote kept), COF ×1 ("Diluted EPS Impact" dropped, second bullet added); EOG ×4 byte-identical to base. Every diff inspected. 15 new tests; full suite 1382 green. The pin is behavioral: on stashed old code the test file errors at collection.

**Repair (same batch):** `scripts/batch185_repair.py` (batch-183 gates), 7 targets: BRO 2024-09-30 (2444→2384), 2024-12-31 (2616→2556), 2025-03-31 (1799→1739), 2025-06-30 (2428→2368), 2025-09-30 (2598→2538), live==blast verified, DB-verified. The stored rows end with the recon heading + wrap tail ("Reconciliation of Commissions and Fees" / "to Organic Revenue") from an older extraction — the repair removes that pair (60 chars/row, both already defect lines); the two orphaned labels appear only in the batch-184 extraction and never reach the DB. BRO 2024-03-31 and 2024-06-30 have no QuarterlyFinancial rows (DB coverage starts 2024-09-30) and are skipped gate-correct. The two labels are added to the script's defect lines (`_BATCH185_ORPHAN_LABELS`) for any stored row that does carry them.

**Related:** Closes the BRO orphaned-label class (batch-184's open item). Still open: AJG's labels ("Net (gains) losses on divestitures" / "Effective income tax rate impact" — prev "3rd Q 24" is not a header-block line); APD 2026-03-31 asterisk-note line in the section path; COR ×6 section-pick refusals; BBY 2026-01-31 "Revenue ($ in millions)" stored line; V Q4 rows and prose rows ×5; ADBE 2025-11-28's section-pick judgment; GPC 2026-03-31's prose-dump stored row; EQIX 2026-03-31's truncated stored headline; the AXP 2024 units-title lines and the DD "Dollars in millions, unless noted" line; the GPC 2025-12-31 alias fragment; the CSX dateline ".1" glue; the lede 6-line budget; the batch-176 31-row audit remainder; AFRM's page-header-prefix false positive; the >1000-char footnotes (Nike 1071, Ford 1179, Coinbase 1262); EMR 2024-06-30's "Copeland Transaction Update" section heading; the standing ABNB/CART/ARCC-table-section-tail/MCO/AFRM/LIN/EOG/SKYD/COIN/pending-filing/Wikipedia/batch-121/CCI/STZ items and the batch-157 gate skips. ## 2026-11-03 — Batch 186: AJG orphaned labels below compact period fragments dropped; CEO quotes now served; 5 stored rows repaired

**What:** A compact-period-fragment predicate (`data/filings.py`) plus a repair run (5/10 rows repaired, 5 skipped gate-correct): `_is_lede_compact_period_fragment` — a whole-line "3rd Q 25" / "9 Mths 25" / "1st Q 26" (ordinal quarter or N months + 2/4-digit year) — joins the header-block disjunction in `_is_lede_flat_table_label`. The second AJG label needs no new rule: once the first enters table mode, the batch-185 mode-residue rule drops it.

**Why:** Arthur J. Gallagher flattens its summary-table column headers one cell per line, and the two reconciliation labels below them ("Net (gains) losses on divestitures", "Effective income tax rate impact") filled the last lede budget lines in 10 quarters, starving the CEO quote — the quarter's actual news (20% revenue growth, 19th straight quarter of double-digit growth in Q3 2025). The bare-period and period-header predicates never fired on the fragments: "3rd"/"Q"/"25" are not period words and the year is 2 digits.

**Design notes:** The predicate is deliberately whole-line-only (fullmatch) and attested AJG-only by a full-cache survey (370 occurrences in lede_lines, all AJG) — the blast confirms the confinement empirically (10 diffs, all AJG, 1,689 byte-identical). The 2026 quarters are a different class and stay untouched: their value rows are dash-led ("— — 84 30 ..."), which the batch-175 `_is_lede_data_row` bullet guard treats as bullets, so the whole summary table is served — fixing that means recognizing dash-led figure-dense rows as data rows, a core-predicate change for its own batch, not folded in here. The pretax paragraph's wrapped lines are table-mode residue (unterminated), so only its terminated tail is served alongside the quote — a wart, but strictly better than the labels, and the naive fix (lowercase-led terminated lines as residue) would eat the CEO quote's own wrapped lines, so it needs a quote-aware rule. The AJG 2025-12-31 repair refusal is a separate pre-existing defect: the batch-160 `_is_lede_table_label` check misfires on the "Summary of Financial Results - Fourth Quarter" heading because its next line ("4th Q 25") counts as a data row — the Q4 table has no "Revenues Before" line between heading and fragments, so only this quarter trips it; the gates correctly refused the repair rather than dropping stored content.

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, `hidden_files/blast186c/base` → `new`): 10 diffs, 1,689 byte-identical, 0 content losses — every removed line is a table label (the two orphaned labels, plus "Risk Management, as adjusted *" where served) and every added line is real narrative (pretax-paragraph tail + CEO quote). Verified programmatically line-by-line. 5 new tests; full suite 1387 green. The pin is behavioral: on stashed old code the test file errors at collection.

**Repair (same batch):** `scripts/batch186_repair.py` (batch-183 gates, `_BATCH186_ORPHAN_LABELS`), 10 targets: AJG 2024-09-30 (840→1711), 2024-12-31 (840→1344), 2025-03-31 (826→714), 2025-06-30 (831→720), 2025-09-30 (840→1579), live==blast verified, DB-verified. The stored rows end in table chrome ("Revenues Before" / "4th Q 25") from an older extraction — already defect lines under the gates — and the stored dateline's webcast half is skipped by the preservation gate's webcast exemption. The 2023 quarters and 2024 Q1/Q2 have no stored rows (DB coverage starts 2024-09-30); AJG 2025-12-31 is skipped gate-correct (heading misclassification above).

**Related:** Closes the AJG orphaned-label class (batch-185's open item). Still open: AJG 2026 dash-led rows; AJG 2025-12-31 heading misclassification; the AJG pretax wrap-tail wart; APD 2026-03-31 asterisk-note line in the section path; COR ×6 section-pick refusals; BBY 2026-01-31 "Revenue ($ in millions)" stored line; V Q4 rows and prose rows ×5; ADBE 2025-11-28's section-pick judgment; GPC 2026-03-31's prose-dump stored row; EQIX 2026-03-31's truncated stored headline; the AXP 2024 units-title lines and the DD "Dollars in millions, unless noted" line; the GPC 2025-12-31 alias fragment; the CSX dateline ".1" glue; the lede 6-line budget; the batch-176 31-row audit remainder; AFRM's page-header-prefix false positive; the >1000-char footnotes (Nike 1071, Ford 1179, Coinbase 1262); EMR 2024-06-30's "Copeland Transaction Update" section heading; the standing ABNB/CART/ARCC-table-section-tail/MCO/AFRM/LIN/EOG/SKYD/COIN/pending-filing/Wikipedia/batch-121/CCI/STZ items and the batch-157 gate skips. ## 2026-11-03 — Batch 187: AJG 2026 dash-led table value rows recognized as data rows; CEO quotes now served; 2 stored rows repaired

**What:** A dash-value-row predicate (`data/filings.py`) plus a repair run (2/2 rows repaired, 0 skipped): `_is_lede_dash_value_row` — a dash-led wordless line whose every token is a dash placeholder or a figure token, with ≥1 figure — is a data row, exempt from the batch-175 bullet guard in both `_is_lede_data_row` and `_is_lede_table_line`. Once the values row counts as a data row, its label line is caught by the batch-160 `_is_lede_table_label` next-line check. No gate change in the repair script: no stored row carries the dash rows or their labels (the stored rows truncate at the table header).

**Why:** Arthur J. Gallagher's 2026 summary tables flatten each adjustment row as a label line plus a values line, and the values line starts with dash cells for the empty revenue columns ("– – 65 33 87 44 0.25 0.13"). `_BULLET_LEAD_RE` matches the dash lead, so the batch-175 bullet guard exempted the whole row — which in turn kept its label from `_is_lede_table_label`, since the next line was not a data row. Both the label and the values then filled the 6-line lede budget as fake bullets, starving the CEO quote — the whole 2026 lede was table chrome (headline + two header lines + dash row + label + dash row + label).

**Design notes:** The predicate is wordless by construction, so it can never be a bullet: a bullet always carries words. The ≥1-figure floor (not ≥2) matters — the "Effective income tax rate impact" values row is "– – – 1 – – – –", a single figure among seven dashes, and a ≥2 floor would have left that label + row in the lede. A dashes-only line is a lone bullet marker, not a value row; a terminated line is prose; a figure-led line is already a data row. The two-guard exemption was forced by the first test run: exempting only `_is_lede_data_row` left the rows served, because `_is_lede_table_line` has its own top bullet guard that returns False before the data-row check is ever reached — the end-to-end test caught it, and the unit shape tests passed throughout, which is why the end-to-end pin matters. The two AJG 2026 header lines ("Summary of Financial Results - First Quarter/Second Quarter", "Net Earnings (Loss) EBITDAC Diluted Net Earnings") are deliberately untouched: the title line carries no "ended" period phrase and no listed table vocab, and the "(Loss)" paren token defeats the table-header capital-led check — each is its own small predicate change with its own blast, not folded in here.

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, `hidden_files/blast187c/base` → `new`): 2 diffs, 1,697 byte-identical, 0 content losses — both AJG 2026, every removed line a table label or dash value row, every added line real narrative (prior-year interest-income paragraph, pretax paragraph, both CEO-quote paragraphs). Verified line-by-line. 5 new tests; full suite 1392 green. The pin is behavioral: on stashed old code the test file errors at collection (`_is_lede_dash_value_row` does not exist).

**Repair (same batch):** `scripts/batch187_repair.py` (batch-183 gates, no gate change), 2 targets: AJG 2026-03-31 (901→2002), 2026-06-30 (900→1687), live==blast verified, DB-verified. The stored rows are truncated older extractions ending at the table header; "Revenues Before" / "Reimbursements" / "Segment" are already defect lines under the gates (lede table lines) and the stored dateline is skipped by the preservation gate's webcast/conference-call exemption.

## 2026-11-04 — Batch 189: abbreviated bare role lines (CEO/CFO/COO) are role lines; AJG Q4 FY2025 quote body served; 1 stored row repaired

**What:** Role-vocabulary fix (`data/filings.py`) plus a repair run (1/1 rows repaired, 0 skipped): `ceo`/`cfo`/`coo` join `_ROLE_TITLE_WORDS` and `_ROLE_ANCHOR_WORDS`, so `_is_bare_role_line` recognizes abbreviated attribution tails ("Chairman and CEO.", "President and CEO.", "CHAIR AND CEO", "CEO and Co-Founder"). The AJG Q4 FY2025 lede ended at the attribution — "We had an excellent fourth quarter and a terrific 2025!" said J. Patrick Gallagher, Jr., / "Chairman and CEO." — and the quote body (20th straight quarter of double-digit growth, 30% revenue growth, 5% organic, margins, the full-year wrap-up) was never served.

**Why:** The attribution is wrapped: the verb + name sit on one line and the bare role on the next. The tail consumed the 6th lede budget line, and the finish rule read it as a complete item — it is a terminated sentence, and the batch-181 split-attribution predicate needs the verb and the role on the same line, so it never fired. Skipping the tail as a bare role line (batch 173's rule) frees the budget line; the quote body then flows in through the batch-188 open-quote finish rule (the body's first line opens a quote that has not closed).

**Design notes:** The abbreviations join the anchor set as well, so a "Group CEO" shape matches; the 2-word floor still keeps a lone "CEO" out, and prose mentioning a role still fails on its non-role words ("John Smith, CEO", "CEO pay ratio disclosure"). The fix is deliberately vocabulary-only — no finish-rule change: the batch-188 open-quote rule already pulls the body once the tail stops consuming the budget. The heading misclassification that started this investigation (batch-160 `_is_lede_table_label` firing on "Summary of Financial Results - Fourth Quarter" above "4th Q 25") is left alone: the batch-188 summary-title branch drops the heading first in the lede loop, so the misclassification is harmless, and exempting compact fragments from `_is_lede_data_row` is a core-predicate change for its own batch.

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, `hidden_files/blast189/base` → `new`): 1 diff, 1,698 byte-identical, 0 content losses — only AJG 2025-12-31, every removed line the role tail and every added line real narrative (the quote body). The other three newly-matching lines change nothing served (spot-checked absent from both base and new outputs). 2 new tests; full suite 1409 green. The pin is behavioral: both new tests fail on stashed old code.

**Repair (same batch):** `scripts/batch189_repair.py` (batch-183 gates, no gate change), 1 target: AJG 2025-12-31 (848→1269), live==blast verified, DB-verified. The stored row was an older extraction truncating after the table header; "Summary of Financial Results - Fourth Quarter" is a defect line via `_is_lede_summary_results_title` and "4th Q 25" via `_is_lede_table_line`, and the stored dateline's webcast second half is skipped by the preservation gate's webcast exemption.

**Related:** Closes the AJG Q4 FY2025 orphaned-quote-body defect (the batch-186/188 carried item's visible symptom). ## 2026-11-04 — Batch 190: compact period fragments are not data rows; AJG heading misclassification closed; 0 stored rows repaired

**What:** Predicate-correctness fix (`data/filings.py`) plus 4 tests, no repair run: `_is_lede_data_row` exempts compact period fragments ("4th Q 25", "9 Mths 25" — AJG's flattened summary-table column headers), and `_is_lede_table_line` gains a compact-fragment branch so the fragment stays table residue. Closes batch 189's carried item (the AJG 2025-12-31 heading misclassification: batch-160 `_is_lede_table_label` firing on "Summary of Financial Results - Fourth Quarter" above "4th Q 25").

**Why:** The fragment's two "figures" are a quarter ordinal and a 2-digit year — period labels, not values — but the density rule counts them (2 of 3 tokens ≥ 40%), so the fragment qualified as a data row and any heading directly above one was misread as a table label. The full-cache survey finds 109 such misfires, all AJG, all table chrome the other rules drop anyway, so the misfire was latent (the batch-188 summary-title branch drops the heading first) — the fix corrects the predicate on its face rather than changing any served content.

**Design notes:** The exemption is in the core predicate, not only in the label check's next-line test — both shapes were simulated over the full cache and both give 0 diffs, but the predicate fix also corrects the repair-gate residue scans (a fragment in a stored row was readable as a data row there). The table-line branch is mandatory, not belt-and-braces: the probe showed the data-row branch was the ONLY branch catching the fragment in `_is_lede_table_line` (period_header False, table-cell False, recon False, single-value False), so exempting without it would have let "4th Q 25" leak into ledes as content. The batch-189 repair gates need no change: a fragment line still blocks there, via `_is_lede_table_line` on the new branch instead of via `_is_lede_data_row`.

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, `/tmp/blast190/base` → `new`): 0 diffs, 1,699 byte-identical, 0 content losses — the first zero-diff blast in the recent run, which is the expected result for a latent-misfire fix (every misfiring line is dropped by other rules either way). 4 new tests, 2 of which fail on stashed old code (behavioral pin); full suite 1413 green. A service restart mid-blast left the fix stashed without a pop; the stash was popped, the fix re-verified (4/4 batch tests), and the blast re-run cleanly.

**Repair (same batch):** none — 0 blast diffs means no stored row changes; the AJG 2025-12-31 row was already repaired in batch 189 and its extraction is unchanged.

**Related:** Closes the AJG heading-misclassification item carried since batch 188/189. ## 2026-11-04 — Batch 191: table-mode paragraph head served with its tail; AJG pretax paragraph whole; 6 stored rows repaired

**What:** A lede-loop fix (`data/filings.py`) plus a repair run (6/6 rows repaired, 0 skipped): new `_lede_wrapped_paragraph_head` scans the table-mode residue run back from its end to the first capital-led line and prepends that suffix when the collected line is the paragraph's terminated tail. The loop tracks the run with a `prev_was_residue` flag — any non-residue branch breaks it — so the run is exactly the consecutive residue-skipped lines since the last table row, header skip, or collected line.

**Why:** Arthur J. Gallagher's pretax paragraph sits inside the summary-table region: its wrapped head lines are unterminated, so the loop skipped them as table-mode residue and only the terminated tail was served — a dangling fragment ("income taxes is shown on pages 14 and 15.") with no subject. The batch-186 note called this a wart and warned the naive fix (lowercase-led terminated lines as residue) would eat the CEO quote's own wrapped lines, so it needed a quote-aware rule; the rule chosen only ever adds lines, and the budget neutrality (only the tail counts) keeps the quote's budget exactly as it was.

**Design notes:** The first implementation required the WHOLE run to be one paragraph and joined nothing — the debug showed the run holds 7 lines at the tail, because the summary table's orphaned labels ("Total Brokerage & Risk Management, as adjusted *", the batch-185/186 chains) are residue-skipped in the same run ahead of the paragraph head. The suffix scan is the fix for that: continuations backward from the run's end, stop at the first capital-led line. A label can never be the head because the head must end mid-sentence (`_LEDE_WRAP_TAIL_RE`: trailing comma/dash or conjunction/preposition/article) while a label ends in "*" or ")" or a noun — the "Total Brokerage & Risk Management, as adjusted *" candidate fails on its "*" ending, and a ≥40-char label ending on a noun fails on the wrap-tail test. A run with no capital-led head joins nothing (the paragraph's true head was consumed by another branch). The year-wrap continuation ("2024, the pretax impact ...") is the only digit-led continuation allowed — any other digit-led line is a table row. The join fires only on the normal-append path, not the overrun path, so a tail collected past the budget keeps today's behavior.

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, `/tmp/blast191/base` → `new`, persisted to `hidden_files/blast191/new`): 10 diffs, 1,689 byte-identical, 0 content losses — all AJG, all pure additions, every added line pretax-paragraph narrative, verified line-by-line; the CEO quote is byte-identical in base and new for all 11 quarters that serve it. 5 new tests; full suite 1418 green. The pin is behavioral: on stashed old code the test file errors at collection (`_lede_wrapped_paragraph_head` does not exist). A service restart interrupted the first blast attempt before it wrote anything; the blast was re-run in separate steps with the fix verified present throughout.

**Repair (same batch):** `scripts/batch191_repair.py` (batch-183 gates, no gate change), 6 targets: AJG 2024-09-30 (1812→2597), 2024-12-31 (1341→2123), 2025-03-31 (1323→2103), 2025-06-30 (1450→2233), 2025-09-30 (1585→2372), 2025-12-31 (1269→2049), live==blast verified, DB-verified. The 2023 quarters and 2024 Q1/Q2 have no stored rows (DB coverage starts 2024-09-30); the 2026 quarters are unchanged (single-line paragraph). The head lines are new in live, not in stored, so no defect-line entry is needed for them.

**Related:** Closes the AJG pretax-paragraph wrap-tail wart carried since batch 186. Still open: AJG 2023-12-31's quote loss (the paragraph consumes the 6-line budget before the quote — needs a budget/priority judgment, not another predicate); C 2024-12-31 (slide-deck exhibit); APD 2026-03-31 asterisk-note line in the section path; COR ×6 section-pick refusals; BBY 2026-01-31 "Revenue ($ in millions)" stored line; V Q4 rows and prose rows ×5; ADBE 2025-11-28's section-pick judgment; GPC 2026-03-31's prose-dump stored row; EQIX 2026-03-31's truncated stored headline; the AXP 2024 units-title lines and the DD "Dollars in millions, unless noted" line; the GPC 2025-12-31 alias fragment; the CSX dateline ".1" glue; the lede 6-line budget; the batch-176 31-row audit remainder; AFRM's page-header-prefix false positive; the >1000-char footnotes (Nike 1071, Ford 1179, Coinbase 1262); EMR 2024-06-30's "Copeland Transaction Update" section heading; the standing ABNB/CART/ARCC-table-section-tail/MCO/AFRM/LIN/EOG/SKYD/COIN/pending-filing/Wikipedia/batch-121/CCI/STZ items and the batch-157 gate skips.

## 2026-11-04 — Batch 192: "#" footnote marker joins _FOOTNOTE_DEF_RE; "Certain results" joins the asterisk-note leads; guidance filter attempted and reverted

**What:** Footnote-definition fix (`data/filings.py`) plus a repair run (1/3 rows repaired, 2 skipped): a `^#\s*\S` shape joins `_FOOTNOTE_DEF_RE` and "Certain results" joins `_ASTERISK_NOTE_LEAD_RE`, so the shared `_footnote_def_block_len` / `_asterisk_note_block_len` machinery drops Air Products' per-share "#" definition and non-GAAP "*" legend on every collection path. APD Q2 FY2026's section tail carried both lines as content; the fix also drops AXP's "# - Denotes a variance ..." legend and the attached APD forms.

**Why:** No footnote predicate covered either line: `_FOOTNOTE_DEF_RE` matches only attached "*"/"†"/"‡" glyphs, and the asterisk-note lead list never had the "Certain results" opener. The "# Per share ..." line is a terminated sentence, so the lede's table-line check returns False on it before any glyph branch; the "*" line is caught by `_LEDE_FOOTNOTE_GLYPH_RE` in the lede loop but the section path's kept-filter only consults the block functions, so it survived there — the section path is the one that serves APD Q2 FY2026.

**Design notes:** The "#" shape is attached-or-spaced, deliberately unlike "*" — the cache survey finds no bullet or prose ever opening with "#" (only definitions, legends, DLR table fragments, and "###" decorations), so the spaced form is safe here where the batch-160 comment pins spaced "*" as not-a-footnote-def. A bare "###" separator opens a block of length 1 in practice (the next line is a heading, never a plausible wrap). The "Certain results" addition stays inside the lead-list pattern: the list is already filer-specific vocabulary ("Measures used" is Allstate's legend), and the "\s*" already covers APD's attached and spaced forms. The freed budget behaves as in batches 166/167: dropping the footnote lines lets the lede/section serve the next real paragraph (APD's sales paragraph, AXP's expenses paragraph) — the blast verifies each addition is genuine prose. The repair needs no gate change: "#" lines are defect lines via `_opens_footnote_def` and the "*" line via `_is_lede_table_line`. One gate subtlety found, not fixed: the stored "Fiscal…" tail line ends in "…", so it reads as terminated and fails the table-line predicate — the APD 2026-03-31 repair skips on it (plus the "-more-" residue); the "-more-" batch will need a truncated-tail exemption.

**Guidance path (attempted, reverted):** `extract_management_guidance` has no footnote handling at all — a post-collection kept-filter (same two block functions) was implemented and blasted over the cache: 216/765 guidance texts change, with 183 added lines, because dropping lines before the 4000-char cap lets later content in (AMT/SPG outlook-table fragments, GE forward-looking-statements risk bullets, FDX's disclaimer, CHD "About" prose). The filter was reverted the same batch: a post-collection filter is the wrong shape for a capped collector — the section collector can use it only because its sections rarely hit the cap, while guidance texts routinely sit at it. The right fix needs section-end analysis (break-vs-skip per exhibit: APD's footnotes sit right after its 2 guidance bullets in some quarters and mid-over-collection in others) and is its own batch, with a guidance blast and stored-guidance repairs (73 guidance texts carry a footnote line; APD has 8 stored guidance rows). Probe scripts kept: `scripts/batch192_guidance_blast.py`, `scripts/batch192_guidance_survey.py`, `scripts/batch192_gblast_analyze.py`.

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, `/tmp/blast192/base` → `new`, persisted to `hidden_files/blast192/new`): 4 diffs, 1,695 byte-identical, 0 content losses — APD 2023-12-31, APD 2025-06-30, APD 2026-03-31, AXP 2023-12-31, every removed line a footnote line and every added line genuine prose, verified line-by-line. 7 new tests, 6 of which fail on stashed old code (behavioral pin); full suite 1425 green.

**Repair (same batch):** `scripts/batch192_repair.py` (batch-183 gates, no gate change), 3 targets: APD 2025-06-30 (1617→1912), live==blast verified, DB-verified. Skipped gate-correct: APD 2024-12-31 (stored carries Guidance bullets the live section-pick does not serve) and APD 2026-03-31 ("Fiscal…" preservation + "-more-" residue).

## 2026-11-04 — Batch 193: guidance collector skips footnote blocks in-loop and ends the section at the next section heading; 113 stored guidance rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `extract_management_guidance`):** the guidance collector was the one path with no footnote handling and it over-collected past the guidance section into results narrative (batch 192's post-collection filter attempt showed the 4000-char cap refills freed budget with next-section content, so the fix had to be in-loop). The collector is now an index loop: footnote-definition / rounding-note / asterisk-note blocks are skipped whole (the section collector's block machinery); a next-section heading (short unterminated title-case line heading a bullet/dateline/long terminated $-less prose block) breaks the section, gated on the section having delivered guidance already (Crown Castle's safe-harbor intro + Restructuring Plan Update precede its table); the anchor tolerates an attached footnote digit on compound headings only and rejects quote fragments; `-more-` and repeated release-title lines are skipped; all-caps period labels no longer break collection; paren-word table headers are not footnote keys; a footnote block never swallows a guidance-vocabulary line. Call/webcast notices break the section (guarded `_CONFERENCE_CALL_NOTICE_RE`, `will host... webcast`, `_is_lede_call_announcement`, `quarterly conference call` table-start). Two more tail shapes came from the repair spot-check: Applied Materials' `Reportable Segment Information` results table (now a table-start alternative) and General Mills' `pre-recorded management remarks ... webcasted question and answer session` notice (new `_CALL_QA_SESSION_RE`, same 120-char guard).

## 2026-11-05 — Batch 194: "-more-" page markers end the highlights lede at the next section heading; truncated-tail repair exemption; 2 stored rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `extract_press_release_highlights`):** the highlights paths never skipped the `-more-` page-continuation marker (the guidance collector gained it in batch 193): the lede loop swallowed it as a table line and the section collector as content, so APD Q2 FY2026 served `-more-` plus the next section's heading as highlights. Both loops now skip `-more-` (never content, never budget) and end the section at a heading-shaped line right after one (`_is_more_page_section_heading`: ≤90 chars, unterminated, title-case, no $/%, not a bullet/dateline/all-caps), when the previously collected line is terminated; a wrapped paragraph across the break and a quote attribution (comma/colon tail) stay collectable, and table mode suppresses the break. A bare company-name page header after the marker is skipped transparently (`_is_trailing_company_signature`: ≤6 capitalized words ending in a corporate suffix — CarMax's `CarMax, Inc.` page-2 header); `_clean_highlights_result` also pops a trailing `-more-` or signature.

**Tests:** 10 in `tests/data/test_filings_batch194.py` (heading/signature shape pins + synthetic lede/continuation/attribution/section/KMX pins + real-exhibit APD 2026-03-31 pin). Pin is behavioral: the test file errors at collection on stashed old code. Full suite: **1470 passed**. `git diff --check` clean.

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, `/tmp/blast194/base` → `new2`, persisted to `hidden_files/blast194/new`): 3 diffs, all correct removals, 0 added lines — APD 2023-09-30 (mid-section `-more-`), APD 2026-03-31 (quote + marker + segment heading), EXPE 2025-09-30 (trailing `Expedia Group, Inc.` signature). 0 `-more-` lines remain in new outputs.

**Repair (same batch):** `scripts/batch194_repair.py` (highlights-specific gates; live fetched from EDGAR via the service): 3 targets, **2 repaired** (APD 2026-03-31 3998→2914, KMX 2026-05-31 1513→1431 — KMX has no cached exhibit), **1 skipped gate-correct** (APD 2024-12-31 section-pick; live drops the stored `Guidance` line). Gates: source matches, live ≥200 chars, live==blast where a blast file exists, no defect lines in live, every stored non-defect ATOMIC line survives in live — the paragraph-level split hid dropped lines behind the first bullet's first 60 chars and the first dry run wrongly "repaired" APD 2024-12-31 to 310 chars, so the gates split on atomic lines. **Truncated-tail exemption:** a stored line ending in `…` counts as preserved when its prefix opens a kept live line, and as a defect line when its prefix opens a line the old live served and the new live drops (APD's stored `Fiscal…` is the cap-truncated dropped heading). Datelines are NOT defect for highlights (legitimate lede content), unlike the batch-193 guidance gates. DB-verified after `--write`.

## 2026-11-05 — Batch 200: guidance section-end misses — colon-ended headings, quote-led followers, results-table period-header followers; 31 stored guidance rows repaired

**Fixes (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `_is_guidance_next_section_heading` + `_is_guidance_section_end_follower`):** three defects, one defect class.
1. **Colon-ended headings:** `_is_sentence_terminated` counts ":" as termination, so "Editor's Notes:", "Message From Our CEO:" and "Caution Concerning Forward Looking Statements:" never read as next-section headings. The heading test now treats a colon-ended line as unterminated (title-case + follower tests still apply). Guidance-word headings and period labels stay exempt.
2. **Quote-led followers:** the follower test's `nxt[0].isupper()` failed on quote-led paragraphs (the opening curly quote is not cased), so a CEO quote after a heading never counted as a follower. It now reads the first cased char. Bonus: Carnival's "Financing and Capital Activity" / "PROPEL: Powering Growth & Returns, Responsibly" headings now end the section (the PROPEL long-term-targets announcement no longer rides inside the FY2026 outlook).
3. **Colon + quotation follower:** Disney's Q2/Q3 FY2025 CEO quotes open with a wrapped fragment (no terminal punctuation on the first flattened line), so even the fixed follower test never fired. **Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `_CALL_HOST_NOTICE_RE` + `_CALL_IN_NUMBERS_RE` + `_notice_starts_sentence`):** the batch-208 carry-forward (CI stored guidance rows carry the conference-call notice block) is closed. Cigna's outlook section is followed by its call notice in a shape none of the batch-193 tests match — "Management will be hosting a conference call ..." (CI x6) and "ConocoPhillips will host a conference call today ..." (COP x2) — neither line carries "webcast"/"replay", so `_CONFERENCE_CALL_NOTICE_RE` never fires. "will host(ing) a conference call" is the discriminator: a guidance sentence points AT the call (DHI's pointer, Biogen's FX line) — it never says the company will host one; "will host its calls" (GE's schedule line) never matches. The guard is sentence-aware (120 chars from the sentence start, the batch-193 number applied to the sentence instead of the line): CI 2024-09-30's notice shares its line with the preceding availability sentence, so the match starts ~180 chars into the line but 11 chars into its own sentence. **Walk-back:** when the notice sentence does not start the break line and the text before it ends with sentence punctuation, unterminated kept lines are popped so the text ends at the last complete sentence instead of a dangling availability fragment ("This quarterly earnings").

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits (`scripts/batch209_cache_blast.py`, `hidden_files/blast209/g_new` vs `blast208/g_new`): **11 diffs, 0 None-flips — 8 pure tail cuts (CI x6 + COP x2) and 3 completions** (the pre-seeding CI quarters 2023-12-31, 2024-03-31, 2024-06-30: the batch-204 lookahead keeps the freed budget lines once the notice break fires inside its window, so the table completes and the disclaimer is served; no stored rows, serve on refresh). 1,688 byte-identical.

**Repair (same batch):** `scripts/batch209_repair.py` (prefix-drop gate — live must equal blast g_new, live lines a byte-identical prefix of stored, every dropped line notice-block content incl. wrapped continuations with no $/% figures): **8 repaired, 0 filled, 0 skipped**. ## 2026-11-07 — Batch 210: table-of-contents anchor rejection in the guidance extractor; DLR over-collection closed

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `_is_toc_entry`):** a heading candidate is a TOC entry when the next line is a bare page number AND the line after that is another title followed by another bare page number — two consecutive (title, page-number) pairs. The anchor is skipped and scanning continues to the real heading. DLR's supplement lists "2025 Outlook" on its contents page (title + page number, then the next entry the same way); anchoring there stored the whole contents page as guidance in all 8 stored DLR rows. The two-pair requirement is the guard: a single bare number after a heading is often just a page footer (ELV's "Financial Guidance Summary" + "6" + "Elevance Health"; SPG's "Guidance Reconciliation" + "43" + "(1)") — neither anchor may be skipped.

**Blast-radius method:** Cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits (`scripts/batch210_cache_blast.py`, `hidden_files/blast210/g_new` vs `g_old`): **12 diffs, 0 None-flips — all DLR, all TOC dumps replaced by genuine Core FFO outlook tables.** 1,687 byte-identical.

## 2026-11-07 — Batch 211: content-aware call-notice discriminator in the Highlights collector; Biogen pointer sentences kept; 0 output changes

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `_is_call_notice`):** the Highlights collector's `_CONFERENCE_CALL_NOTICE_RE` tail-match hazard (carried since batch 193) is closed. The bare regex fires on any line mentioning "conference call" before "webcast"/"replay", so a genuine content sentence whose tail only points AT the call is treated as a notice — dropped from the lede or ending the Highlights section. Biogen's FX line ("Other modeling considerations will be provided on the conference call and webcast.", 12 quarters) is the attested shape. The discriminator is the pointer preposition ("on the/its/our conference call"): a notice ANNOUNCES the call, it never says content will be provided ON it. Both Highlights uses (lede skip, section break) now go through the helper; the guidance collector keeps its batch-193 line guard. The rejected alternatives are the finding: a 120-char sentence-start guard cannot separate Biogen (match 63 chars into its short pointer sentence) from CSCO's notice sentence (18 chars in), and a scheduling-verb test cannot separate Biogen from the CSCO/FDX notices (no host verb in either) — the pointer preposition is the only discriminator that separates them, and it occurs in exactly the 12 Biogen lines across all 1,699 cached exhibits.
